definition{
  name: excessOver,
  parameters: [threshold : Nat, observed : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (prim subtract observed threshold : Nat)
}

theorem{
  name: noExcessIsMet,
  parameters: [threshold : Nat, observed : Nat],
  statement: (((excessOver threshold observed) = 0) -> (observed <= threshold)),
  proof: linear_arithmetic{definitions: [excessOver]},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: metHasNoExcess,
  parameters: [threshold : Nat, observed : Nat],
  statement: ((observed <= threshold) -> ((excessOver threshold observed) = 0)),
  proof: linear_arithmetic{definitions: [excessOver]},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: excessFallsWithTheObservation,
  parameters: [threshold : Nat, before : Nat, after : Nat],
  statement: ((after <= before) -> ((excessOver threshold after) <= (excessOver threshold before))),
  proof: linear_arithmetic{definitions: [excessOver]},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: excessRisesOnlyWithTheObservation,
  parameters: [threshold : Nat, before : Nat, after : Nat],
  statement: (((excessOver threshold before) < (excessOver threshold after)) -> (before < after)),
  proof: linear_arithmetic{definitions: [excessOver]},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: requestThresholdIsTheTableLimit,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((project (measureRouteRequestBytes) threshold) = (Routes::requestLimit)),
  proof: decide{}
}

definition{name: selfProjectionObservedMilliseconds, parameters: [], result: Nat, body: 6256}

theorem{
  name: selfProjectionHasNoExcess,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((excessOver (project (measureSelfProjectionMilliseconds) threshold) (selfProjectionObservedMilliseconds)) = 0),
  proof: decide{}
}
